# KOTOR 2: super-deep character write-ups (Oct 19, 2026)

Method: for every character, a script pulled ALL of their own lines and every line by others that names them, from raw/kotor/dialog/ALL2.tsv (45,051 transcribed lines, including 19,180 player dialogue options). It also pulled every walkthrough-narration line naming them, from the saved page text (digest/K2-narr.txt), and the gameplay stat blocks from the raw .wiki pages. Near-duplicate he/she variants are collapsed. ALL own lines are included regardless of length. Only exact duplicates and he/she variants are merged, and each merge is noted inline. 'About' lines cover names, aliases and epithets (chargen/aliases.py) plus pronoun references resolved from context (scene-wide, every candidate hand-reviewed and flagged [context-resolved] with its anchor line). A separate bank lists lines addressed to the character. The hand-written synthesis comes first. The quote banks follow it, verbatim and grouped by scene (page). Every «quote» is checked by verify_kotor_quotes.py. Game mechanics are flagged as mechanics. (RCM) marks Restored Content Mod material.

Characters (24): The Exile (player character); Kreia / Darth Traya; Darth Nihilus; Darth Sion; Atton Rand; Visas Marr; Mandalore (Canderous Ordo); The Handmaiden (Brianna); The Disciple (Mical); Mira; Hanharr; G0-T0 (Goto); Bao-Dur; HK-47 (KOTOR 2); T3-M4 (KOTOR 2); Atris; Master Vrook Lamar; Master Kavar; Master Zez-Kai Ell; Vandar Tokare (KOTOR 2 cameo); Master Lonna Vash; Colonel Tobin and General Vaklu; Azkul, Visquis and Loppak Slusk; Revan (absent, KOTOR 2)

## The Exile (player character)
*Corpus coverage: 8677 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 271 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 0 pronoun references resolved from context · 2701 lines addressed to them · 41 narration lines · 1795 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
The Exile was a Jedi who followed Revan to the Mandalorian Wars. Atris: «You followed Revan to war against the Mandalorians.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]. The Exile served as a General; HK-47: «You were a General in the Mandalorian Wars» [K2/HK-47]. Fought at Dxun and Serroco, per Atton and Sion.
At Malachor V the Exile gave the command to fire Bao-Dur's mass shadow generator. Bao-Dur: «the damage I did» [K2/Depths]. Thousands died, and the Exile survived only by cutting off the Force. Kavar: «You cut yourself off, because you had to if you were to survive.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave].
Unlike Revan, the Exile returned to the Council and was exiled. Kavar: «it was as if the Force had died within you, leaving you hollow» [K2/Royal_Palace]. Atris later baited the Sith with the Exile's return [K2/Telos].
### Personality and motivations
Player-determined. The constants: a quiet veteran with a gift for bonds. Vrook: «your ability to form such connections, to influence others, was always a subject of discussion» [K2/Crystal_Cave]. Mandalore credits the Exile's leadership: «You brought tactics, backbone, and above all else, victory» [K2/Mandalore].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Peragus:** wakes in the medbay, escapes with Atton and Kreia, and begins to reconnect to the Force.
- **Telos:** the TSF, the Restoration Zone, then Atris on the Polar Plateau. She sends the Exile to find the remaining Masters.
- **Four-planet hunt:** Nar Shaddaa (Zez-Kai Ell, Goto), Dxun and Onderon (Kavar, Mandalore), Dantooine (Vrook), Korriban (Sion and the visions).
- **Rebuilt Enclave:** the Masters try to strip the Exile's Force connection; Kreia intervenes and drains them.
  - **Dark path:** if the Exile killed the Masters earlier, there is an alternative scene.
- **Telos:** defeats Atris.
- **Ravager:** kills Nihilus with the party.
- **Malachor:** breaks Sion; kills Traya.
- **Ending:** alignment-dependent.
### Relationships
- **Kreia:** teacher, bond, and finally enemy.
- **Atton:** loyal pilot; romance for a female Exile.
- **Visas:** follower; the Exile is her "echo".
- **Handmaiden** (male Exile) or **Disciple** (female Exile).
- **Bao-Dur:** wartime engineer under the General.
- **Mandalore:** old enemy.
- **Masters:** judges.
### Abilities (lore)
- The "wound in the Force". Traya: «no Jedi ever made the choice you did. To sever ties so completely, so utterly, that it leaves a wound in the Force.» [K2/Trayus_Core].
- Feeds on death and strengthens through bonds. Zez-Kai Ell: «You feed on it, and you grow stronger.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave].
- Resists Nihilus's drain [K2/Bridge narration].
- Withstood Malachor's echo. Atris: «You were strong enough to withstand it once» [K2/Telos].
### Feats
- Malachor V command (war).
- Survived Malachor's mass death [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave].
- Defeats Sion four times on Malachor, breaking his will [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)].
- Defeats Atris [K2/Telos].
- Nihilus's drain backfires on the Exile: "Nihilus raises his hand to drain you, but suddenly reels back, collapsing on the ground" [K2/Bridge]. Then kills him with the party.
- Defeats Traya [K2/Trayus_Core].
- Dark path: kills Kavar (Tobin: «Master Kavar is no match for you» [K2/Royal_Palace]), Vrook and Zez-Kai Ell.
### Power-scaling statements (key)
- «Your single life changed the face of the galaxy, of history itself.» (HK-47) [K2/HK-47]
- «few have your strength in such matters» (Atris) [K2/Telos]
- «Your leadership and skill towers over your companions'.» (Tobin) [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Traya's endorsements (first-pass notes): «You are greater than any I have ever trained.»
### Duels and fights
| Fight | Page | Outcome and context |
|---|---|---|
| vs Sion (Korriban) | K2/Sith_Academy | Retreat; Sion restores himself. |
| vs Atris | K2/Telos | Exile wins. |
| vs Nihilus | K2/Bridge | Exile wins with the party; drain backfire; Visas assists or sacrifices herself. |
| vs Sion (Malachor) | K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top) | Four defeats; his will breaks. |
| vs Traya | K2/Trayus_Core | Exile wins. |
| vs Masters (dark path) | various | Exile wins. |
### Verdict implications
- **Matchups:** above Sion (high), Traya (medium-high, in-game), Nihilus (medium in-game; low-medium for a neutral rematch).
- **Tier placement:** tentatively tier 5 below Revan.
- **Counterarguments:** party assistance; bond-based power growth; Traya wanted to lose.
- **Confidence:** medium.
### Open questions
- Gender and name are player-set.
- The exact Malachor event (who "pressed the button") is described only as the General's command.

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- No NPC stat block on the wiki (party member or player character; party stats live on the class pages).

### Cut content (RCM)
- None found on the RCM-tagged lines for this character.

- (Player choice 2.) «I want my property - my slaves and my ship - returned.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

- (Player choice 9.) «[[../Polar Plateau#Revenge» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «The Force brought me here.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «Refresh my memory - why did the Council cast me out?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

- (Player choice 2.) «I did so for the Republic's sake, not Revan's.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 5.) «The Council wanted to assess the threat, while people were dying by the millions.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 6.) «Better to go to war than be ruled by cowardice.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 7.) «I'd follow Revan over the Jedi any day.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 5.) «I could not stand by and allow innocents to die in the Outer Rim.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 7.) «I deserved exile. I was no longer a Jedi.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «The Council exiled me out of fear... of Revan, of me, and of what we represented.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «Maybe you should admit that it was the Council's decisions that led to the Jedi Civil War.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 6.) «They were right - Jedi do not go to war, no matter what the reasons.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «I also recall you wished me imprisoned... or worse.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «I remember now, you do not need to remind me again.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «My punishment could have been worse. For that, I am grateful.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «I realize now you and the Council were merciful.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «That's my lightsaber!» [K2/Polar_Plateau] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- (Player choice 2.) «I was no longer a Jedi... it was no longer mine.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «It was your right to do so - I no longer deserved to.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «I did not realize you still had it after so many years.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 5.) «Kept it? Stole it, you mean.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «I wish to earn the right to carry my lightsaber again.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «If I became a Jedi again, would you return it to me?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

- (Player choice 4.) «Insult to the Order? You insult me by carrying it.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

- (Player choice 1.) «I had no other choice but to go to war!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «I went to war to protect others, not for battle.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «And the Council's way was to meet aggression with surrender? I'll take my choice any day.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «Is the Jedi way to let people be massacred?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «I told you. It was not aggression - I was defending others.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «It was for a just cause.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 5.) «The Mandalorians were butchering innocent worlds, worlds that we had sworn to protect.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «The Council was weak and ruled by fear.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «It was my choice, and I chose battle - gladly.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «If being a Jedi means surrendering, I choose not to be a Jedi.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «The Mandalorians deserved to be met in battle.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «Were you not tempted to help the innocent?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «I did not lose myself there - I found myself.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 5.) «I lost nothing in the war.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «I would make the same choice again.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «Revan and Malak turned on you, not I.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «Then you know nothing of what drove me... or Revan.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «If the Jedi had backed us, there never would have been a Civil War!» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «Abandoning fellow Jedi fighting a just war is not my way.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «So you were content to let the Outer Rim die? For the sake of teachings?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «Tell that to the millions who died in the war... lives we could have saved, if the Jedi had acted earlier.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «Our teachings do not mean we should stand by and watch others die.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «Hesitation and passivity are also strong emotions - ones we should guard against.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «If we had not fought, the Republic would have fallen. That is a fact.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «Even the Mandalorians admit that Revan turned them back.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «No, there was no guarantee we would win. But if we had not acted, the Mandalorians would now rule the Republic.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 5.) «Have you been reading the history of the blind? Revan. Won. The. War.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «We could have waited, but defeating the Mandalorians after they had won would have been difficult.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «...the triumph of pacifism? Surrender?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «Your words would have rung hollow if the Mandalorians had crushed the Republic and conquered the galaxy.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «If we had not acted, thousands of worlds would have been pillaged and burned of life.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «Nice words... but if we listened to you, we'd be hearing it in Mandalorian right now.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «If you trust only in the Force to settle events and disregard the need to act, then the Mandalorians would rule the galaxy.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «And how many people would have died if the Mandalorians hadn't been stopped?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «If we hadn't stopped them, the Republic would have been destroyed.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «The triumph of the Jedi teachings is a cold thing when there is no one left alive to appreciate them.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 1.) «If the Mandalorians had won, would the Jedi have fought then? Or simply have meditated on what to do?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 2.) «Oh, please. Anyone who stepped outside the Jedi Archives would know it.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 3.) «I have only my experiences to guide me - and they tell me you and the Council were wrong.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- (Player choice 4.) «You are correct - I do not know. And neither do you.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

- (Player choice 4.) «You only delay the inevitable, Sith Lord. Surrender, and I shall spare you.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

- (Player choice 4.) «[[../Trayus Academy#Simple» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- (Player choice 1.) «Because I was able to turn away from it. And you could not.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

- (Player choice 2.) «To face death and keep standing - it leaves scars, yes. But it leaves room to heal.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

- (Player choice 3.) «It is possible to live without the Force. And die without it.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]

### Quote bank B: what others say about The Exile (player character) (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K2/Administration_Level]**
- Atton: «Just in case you don't understand the trouble we're in, let me lay it out for you. This facility is a series of mining tunnels dropping kilometers into the asteroid's core. Every worker and droid here has been cleared, then triple-cleared, for hazardous operations. This facility has an operational threat level so high they don't let un-cleared starships in system out of fear that an accidental engine discharge will create a very brief, very hot new sun in this sector. You know why? Because every asteroid in this orbiting minefield has deposits of Peragian fuel within it - that means when one decides to pop, its brothers and sisters are going to come along for the ride. One explosion - you might survive. But a hundred? A thousand? You'll be riding out of here on a comet trail, Jedi. So if something's gone wrong here, you can cling to the Force all you want, but nothing'll stop this place from going critical.» [K2/Administration_Level]

**[K2/Atton]**
- Atton: «Of course you did. You see, Jedi... light or dark... do it, more often than you'd think. But I never heard one say they were sorry before... that's a new house rule.» [K2/Atton]
- Atton: «I did. Up until the Republic officers began to "betray" their oaths to the Republic and side with Revan - Admiral Karath, Mon Halan, General Derred, and all the rest.» [K2/Atton]

**[K2/Bao-Dur]**
- Bao-Dur: «Hey, General. Are you all right?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Hey, General. I want to talk to you.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «You can't hide it, General. Just look at your face. I saw other Jedi who looked like you - during and after the war. It's that woman. You have to get away from her. She'll destroy you.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «I'm not saying you're not strong enough, General, but I'm not discounting her either.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Just be careful, General. You don't know what she wants, and she's got some goal. I'm sure of it.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «All I wanted to do was send a message, but I couldn't even do that right. That's the past, though. It's good to be working with you again, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «It's good to be working with you again, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Yes, General?» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Sure, it's nothing important, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «You know, I'm glad I found you again, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Only if you don't have any more questions, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «I'll be sure to do that, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «They might not call you a Jedi anymore, but believe me, you are. It's not the sort of thing that you just stop being. You're stuck with it, just like you're stuck being the General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Having you here has an effect on me, General. I never noticed it years ago. I think my mind was too occupied then.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Yes, teach me the ways of the Force, General. Together, our enemies will fall.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «We can't all be Jedi like you, General.» [K2/Bao-Dur]
- Bao-Dur: «Never, General. It had to be done. My hands destroyed the Mandalorians. I cannot be forgiven for that.» [K2/Bao-Dur]

**[K2/Battle_Circle]**
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «You have violated the rules of the battle circle! Have you no honor, Jedi?» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «Put your weapon away, Jedi. Don't step out of the circle, and pull any death blow. No one dies in the battle circle. So put away your weapon and step into the battle circle. Now you will experience a true fight.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «The match between the Jedi and Davrel is over. Davrel is the clear and honorable victor. You see, Jedi? Our training is rigorous, and is quite different from yours. With us, anything could be a weapon. Even hand and foot could be lethal. I'm sure Davrel would gladly challenge you again. He is eager to gain honor, even from you.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «I will send a runner to Kex. Prepare yourself for your fight, Jedi.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «This is a great honor, Jedi. Kelborn is one of our former champions. He has declared you worthy enough to fight the best among us. If you choose to fight again, you will face him. If you somehow beat Kelborn, then you will take on Bralor.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Kelborn: «No, I can't. We all have our orders, Jedi.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Kelborn: «You are that eager for a rematch? So am I, Jedi. You're more of a challenge than most of my brothers. Talk to the sergeant, I'm sure it can be arranged.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «The match between the Jedi and Kelborn is over. Kelborn is the clear and honorable victor. You are a strong warrior, Jedi, but no match for the best of us. If you take away your lightsaber and Force, then our training techniques can be more than a match for you.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Bralor: «This is not a battlefield, Jedi. If it were, I trust you wouldn't have the same opinion. I prepare and train, readying my mind and weaponry for a real challenge. Peace is not our natural element. Mandalorians handle it differently. I focus on educating myself in the martial techniques I will need to succeed in the next war.» [K2/Battle_Circle]
- Mandalorian Sergeant: «The match between the Jedi and Bralor is over. Bralor is the clear and honorable victor. You did not win, Jedi, but I do not know how well Bralor would fare in a rematch.» [K2/Battle_Circle]

**[K2/Bridge]**
- Admiral Onasi: «I've read your records, how the Jedi sentenced you. For doing what you believed. You wandered past the Outer Rim during your exile. I ask you... did you find any trace of Revan?» [K2/Bridge]
- Admiral Onasi: «Safe journey, exile.» [K2/Bridge]
- Admiral Onasi: «He asked us to stay, to keep the Republic safe. It was important to him. And after meeting the exile, I'm convinced that there are worse things to lose in the galaxy.» [K2/Bridge]

**[K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]**
- Loppak Slusk: «You've got a lot of nerve coming here, Jedi, with the bounty we've got on your kind. What do you want?» [K2/Bumani_Exchange_Corp.]

**[K2/Cantina]**
- Luxa: «I think you're even tougher than you look, Jedi or not.» [K2/Cantina]

**[K2/Crystal_Cave]**
- Bao-Dur: «Nice job, General.» [K2/Crystal_Cave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Kreia: «That can hardly be avoided. Even mundane actions of the basest creatures can create detectable ripples in the Force. A mynock beating its wings on the plains could cause a sandstorm in the desert. And figuratively speaking, Jedi have much bigger wings.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «You've just confirmed everything I thought about you. You were a stain to the reputation of the Jedi Order. I may have been caged, but I was never helpless. Guard yourself, exile.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]
- Master Vrook: «Because you defied the Council and followed Revan to war. And from that war, came another. Do you know how many worlds were destroyed in those wars - Dantooine? Telos? Serroco? Do you see now why we counseled caution, instead of action? You were the only one to come back and face judgment. But that was because you had lost your connection to the Force - another casualty of war. No doubt you still blame us for your exile - I had hoped your isolation would give you time to reflect on what had happened, what you had done, but I see it has not.» [K2/Crystal_Cave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Master Vrook: «You've become strong again... but I know not how. This isn't over, exile.» [K2/Crystal_Cave]

**[K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]**
- Azkul: «I'm not your friend, Jedi. If you're looking for stories, go read a datapad.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «I see you have found the elusive Master Vrook. He's told me that his "rescue" complicated our situation to some degree. I can't say that I anticipated that. I thank you for finding him, though. Vrook has informed me that the mercenaries devised a plan of attack to annihilate Khoonda itself. I must ask for your aid again, Jedi.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Terena Adare: «Do you require anything else, Jedi?» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «So, Jedi. I take it you are responsible for the surprises we've had during this battle. Stand aside. Our quarrel is with the distinguished Administrator. This isn't your problem.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «[Failure] Is that supposed to convince me, Jedi? I was trained by the Sith. My unit killed many Jedi. I'll give you one last chance. Leave the Administrator to her fate.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «That was my final offer, Jedi. Let's see the value of your boasts.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «You should have let him live. He would have fetched a fine price on Nar Shaddaa. Khoonda is ours, thanks in part to your assistance. Here, take your reward, Jedi.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «[Failure] Pathetic, Jedi. Try your mind tricks on weak-minded fools like yourself. Take these credits and go!» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «You're free to go, Jedi. Just don't cause any trouble for us, or I will collect on that bounty.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]
- Azkul: «Our business is concluded, Jedi.» [K2/Defense_of_Khoonda]

**[K2/Depths]**
- Hanharr: «Words will not break the shackles on my wrists. Words will not end the hatred I have for you. My hatred for you would drown the raging storms of this planet. It burns hotter than the hatred in the heart of the gray-maned woman... and greater than her feelings for the exile.» [K2/Depths]
- Bao-Dur: «If you are watching this, then what the General suspected was true... the Sith are striking from the graveyard of Malachor V. The ship the Sith Lord was on was one of the vessels damaged in that final battle. If you reach Malachor's surface, the mass shadow generator can be undone... provided there are enough vessels intact within the orbiting debris or buried in the planet's surface. Use the sequence I am unlocking in your memory banks on the engine core of any vessel you encounter on Malachor. If my calculations are correct, four vessels should be enough to power up the mass shadow generator and undo the damage that was done in Malachor V... the damage I did. Once the sequence has begun, it cannot be stopped, so make sure the General is off the planet before triggering the sequence. Otherwise, there will be no retreat. I know your weapon systems are minimal, and I don't know what awaits you on Malachor's surface. Try to avoid contact with any potential threats. Follow your programming...» [K2/Depths]
- Bao-Dur: «If you are hearing this, then the sequence has been completed... when active, it will destroy Malachor... again. I have one last command for you. You must remain behind and ensure that the sequence fires properly once the General gives the command... if not, then all we have fought for has been for nothing. You have done all I asked... all you were built for. And for that, I thank you.» [K2/Depths]
- G0-T0: «Touching. The probability of the Iridonian installing triggered commands within your core was high; I see the probabilities have played out. Of course, the probability that I would do the same is equally high. Your inability to move right now is evidence of that. If the General issues the command, only I will be here to receive it. You realize I cannot permit you... or the exile... to activate the mass shadow generator here on Malachor. In that, your programming and mine... conflict. And since you have no offensive weaponry to speak of, the probability of your programming overwriting mine is low. You must understand that the General would not wish the relics or the Sith strength here on Malachor to be compromised. Their presence is needed to stabilize the galaxy. Without them, the galaxy would be reduced to anarchy within years. And if there is anything I can't stand, it's an untidy galaxy. So, let us wait here, you and I, for the General's orders, and the fate of the galaxy shall wait with us.» [K2/Depths]

**[K2/Disciple]**
- Disciple: «Forgive me, but there is something I must ask. In my study of the Jedi histories and the more... contemporary records, I have heard tales of a Jedi who was exiled. You are that Jedi, but the records are somewhat evasive on why this was done. I wanted to discuss why you chose to leave the Jedi Order and accept exile.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «The record of the exile's trial - I wish to see it.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Yes, but exile is rare, and I have found it is not really something that the order can enforce. Believe it or not, it was really your choice.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Did you know that exile is a rare sentence? It is not really something that the order can enforce. Believe it or not, it was really your choice.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «Atton... I feel that perhaps there has been some miscommunication between us concerning the exile.» [K2/Disciple]
- Kreia: «Jedi.» [K2/Disciple]
- Disciple: «No. It is something of a mystery why they would exile themselves as they have. It is not the way of the Jedi to vanish in such a way... especially when the Republic is in need of them. I fear that there is something else at work, something that we cannot see. Then again, perhaps the Jedi are hiding simply because so many people hate them these days.» [K2/Disciple] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Dormitories]**
- HK-50 Assassin Droid: «Chiding Answer: My programming renders me incapable of revealing the indentity of my client, master. However, I am free to say that my client is wealthy, and very interested in possessing the last of the Jedi.» [K2/Dormitories]

**[K2/Enclave_Courtyard]**
- Kreia: «That is no exaggeration, Jedi holocrons are tools of instruction, of value to any who study the Force. If this... *man* has one, it is worth his price.» [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]

**[K2/Enclave_Sublevel]**
- Disciple: «Admiral, this is Mical. I have found the exile.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]
- Gerevick: «And now, Jedi, we shall fight.» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]

**[K2/Goto's_Yacht]**
- Twin Suns: «Finally, we meet the exile. You should tell your companion that he should strike to kill, not wound.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «So you have returned from exile, though I do not know why. You were always difficult to read, when you were tied to the Force, and even more when it was lost to you. But I can sense the death of others upon your hands - I know how this will end. But I will answer your questions, if only for some measure of peace. In exchange, I wish only the answer to one. Why have you done this to the Jedi? Was it just revenge - or something more?» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «So you have returned from exile. Kavar thought you might, if only to wander your old battlegrounds. But I did not think you would come to Nar Shaddaa. Still, you were always a difficult one to read - both when you were tied to the Force, and even more when it was lost to you.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «Yes, she holds the last of the Jedi teachings... it is good she survived.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «It is a long story, but there is no harm in you knowing. And someone should know. Only a handful of us remained after the Jedi Civil War, barely a hundred in number. Then even that hundred began to vanish, in places where the Force seemed blind. The only pattern we determined that when Jedi gathered, they were seen no more. At the last Jedi conclave on the Miraluka world of Katarr, the entire planet was wiped out. An entire race, destroyed... because the Jedi chose to gather there. It was only then that we realized we were facing something far more powerful than we knew how to fight.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «...I can only imagine what it must have been like for you. But even that imagining cannot compare with the truth. But there is more than that. On Nar Shaddaa... one cannot escape what was left from the Jedi Civil War. From the failure of the Masters, from our failure to properly train Jedi, came disaster. And I wondered, if perhaps, the teachings of the Jedi had been our failing all along. There have been so many failures, by teachers who believed in the Code with all their being - Master Arca failed Ulic, as Master Baas failed Exar Kun, as Kae and Zhar and the others of the Council failed Revan... and Malak. For all the acts we do to preserve the galaxy, from such an arrogance that all we do is right and just, I wonder if there is a counter-effect that is created, that strikes back at us. Exar Kun, Ulic Qel-Droma, Malak, Revan, you... all Jedi. There is something wrong in the Force, a wound, a sound that is growing, like a scream. You can hear it echo on Nar Shaddaa, sometimes when the moon is on orbit. It is a frightening thing to feel, that perhaps being connected to all life is not enlightenment at all, but simply another doom. And I think that maybe, perhaps, to forsake the Force as you did, to cut loose our bonds, may not be the wrong thing to do. You taught me something important in the Council Chamber long ago, exile, and it has stayed with me all these years. You were right to do what you did. Everything you did.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «Now... now I must take up the role I was ready to cast aside. This threat has finally revealed itself, and we Jedi will need to stand together. I did not speak fully of what I have felt. Staying on Nar Shaddaa - it is an exile of sorts. One that I have chosen. I, too, lost a Padawan on Malachor. Not to the battle, but to the alternative - to the teachings that Revan brought from the Unknown Regions. And I was not the only Jedi Master to watch a student turn on them.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «Perhaps exile has been good to you indeed - it has certainly not dulled your instincts, nor the speed at which you learn. I shall go to Dantooine, to the ruins of the Enclave. If you gather the others, I will meet you there. And... thank you, exile. Your returning... it is good that you are back among us.» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]

**[K2/HK-47]**
- HK-47: «Answer: Revan saw the pressures of war on Dxun, and knew that even in the heat of conflict, Jedi could be broken, not physically, but psychologically.» [K2/HK-47]
- HK-47: «Jedi, like sand-kivers, seem to sense trouble a few seconds before it happens. They are tricky little pests.» [K2/HK-47]

**[K2/Handmaiden]**
- Handmaiden: «Oh... welcome, exile. Is there something you need?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «You honor me, exile. Together, then.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «There is much knowledge on Telos, and only one of the Jedi remain. There is so much about their ways of battle, their forms, their stances, that may be lost forever if the last of the Jedi is taken from the galaxy.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «You will take back that challenge, exile. If you do not, then I shall teach you not to make such accusations lightly.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I see. Thank you, exile. I appreciate you sharing your knowledge with me.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Handmaiden: «Very well - forgive me, exile, I did not mean to speak of things painful to you.» [K2/Handmaiden] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Kreia: «Never have you wondered what it would mean in the Echani rituals if the two of you sparred and fought - and you won, completely and utterly? If perhaps she would give in, surrender herself to you? Few are the thoughts that can hide in the shadows of your mind, exile... and such passions are not strength, but erosion.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Handmaiden: «I will not let you down, exile. I will honor you, as I honor the face of my mother.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Atris: «She has betrayed me... the last of your sisters has betrayed me. Betrayed us. She is your sister no more. She does not travel with the exile - instead, she has chosen to walk the path of the Jedi.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Visas: «You are alone and wounded in your own way. I can feel its pulse, like a heartbeat from the past. But if you will not believe that I mean him no harm, then believe this - I wish to learn from him.  I wish to learn how it is he still walks when his spirit is nothing more than a shell... when you feel as though the Force, as if life itself, has abandoned you.  I had a question for you, sister of the Echani. The exile... what does he look like?» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «I have seen what you have seen - the slow seduction of the Sith. Perhaps we are more allies than you know. I fear the exile has let his feelings for the Miraluka affect his judgment - and it will doom him.» [K2/Handmaiden]
- Kreia: «What you think is of no concern - what the exile thinks is what should concern us.» [K2/Handmaiden]

**[K2/Hangar_Bay]**
- Kreia: «The Republic warship was the Harbinger. It was seized on its way to Telos by the Sith - they sought you, Jedi.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Kreia: «Because you are the last of the Jedi. Once you are dead, then they have won.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Kreia: «Exile or not - the Sith believe you to be a Jedi Knight, and that is all that matters.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Kreia: «Before the war, Jedi who failed their training were sent to the fields of Telos, to serve the galaxy... not as Jedi Knights, but as farmers and laborers. The destruction of Telos was complete - I doubt any Jedi remain. Yet there may be... echoes of their passing. We shall see.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]

**[K2/Hanharr]**
- Hanharr: «I believe you are correct, human - and so wrong, at the same time. It is enough, I think. You have taken me prisoner, but you do not own me. I see the slaves you have gathered around you. You are as much a slaver as the tribe of Czer-ka, but the price of that slavery lies behind the eyes. In the heart. Do not think we are different, you and I. You are as much of a monster, an exile, as I am. We both walk in the Shadowlands, to prove ourselves great.» [K2/Hanharr]
- Hanharr: «When I die, I shall become part of the Shadowlands... and never return from my exile. This blackness that surrounds me will be where I die, forever.» [K2/Hanharr]

**[K2/Influence]**
- Bao-Dur: «And that is why I follow you, General. Lead on.» [K2/Influence]
- Mira: «Well, that's not something you see everyday. Guess they can't take the Jedi out of the exile, can they?» [K2/Influence]
- Bao-Dur: «You did the right thing, General.» [K2/Influence]
- Handmaiden: «I had not expected such an act from an exile.» [K2/Influence]
- Atton: «You really believe that? Look, Jedi or not, you can't treat the galaxy as one big charity case - if you do, then the galaxy's going to step all over you.» [K2/Influence]
- Bao-Dur: «As always, General, when you lead, I shall follow. But it is only because we have been through so much together.» [K2/Influence]
- Bao-Dur: «That was not a battle we needed to fight, General.» [K2/Influence]

**[K2/Iziz_Cantina]**
- Queen Talia: «Is this necessary? I can guarantee your safety in the Palace, but outside these walls the General has many men that would do anything for him.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Colonel Tobin: «Appearances have to be maintained. And I'm afraid we don't have much time. You've helped out General Vaklu, and he understands how to show gratitude. We have allies, terrible allies. They want you dead. However, the General believes you could ultimately be an even greater ally. But we have to maintain appearances. Do you understand?» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]
- Mandalore: «It's up to you, Jedi. This soldier knows the terrain, and can get us where we need to go quickly... without needing to blast our way through herds of cannoks.» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]**
- Visquis: «Let all the clientele know the Jedi... the real Jedi... has arrived. If necessary, remind them of the price for the exile's capture.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]
- Visquis: «Hanharr, do not take me for a fool... they cannot kill the exile, only weaken her for capture.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Visquis: «The exile has come far. Too far.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr]

**[K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]**
- Visquis: «So you are the exile mentioned in the coreward database. Strange... I had not thought even an ex-Jedi would stoop to such depravities.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Visquis: «So you are the exile mentioned in the coreward database. It seems my squeezing of the Refugee Sector has yielded success, after all.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Hanharr: «You want me to hunt the human Jeedai, the exile - and kill h-» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Kreia: «No, that you shall not do. You will not bring harm to the exile - and if you do, beast, I shall break you. Even your madness will not save you if you bring harm to the exile. Know this.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Kreia: «All it requires is that you immerse yourself in another lie, beast. The exile - you shall be her servant, until I call upon you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]
- Kreia: «All it requires is that you immerse yourself in another lie. The exile - you shall be his servant, until I call upon you.» [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]

**[K2/Jungle]**
- Mira: «Watch it, Jedi. There's a body over there. A lone Mandalorian. Fresh kill, too. I can't figure the percentage in them still being on this rock.» [K2/Jungle]
- Davrel: «You finally arrive, Jedi. I seek to reclaim the honor that you stole from me. I challenge you here, outside of the battle circle. This time we fight to the death!» [K2/Jungle]
- Davrel: «Then you dishonor me again, Jedi. One day we will meet again... Next time you will not have a choice.» [K2/Jungle]
- Handmaiden: «Even though Mandalorians were your sworn enemy, you would still save one in need? You are a perplexing man, Exile.» [K2/Jungle]

**[K2/Jungle_Landing]**
- Mira: «Sweet talk isn't going to make me change my mind about you, Jedi.» [K2/Jungle_Landing]

**[K2/Khoonda]**
- Terena Adare: «What have you learned, Jedi?» [K2/Khoonda]

**[K2/Khoonda_Plains]**
- Dillan: «You'll find little welcome here, Jedi.  For your own good, I recommend you speak to Administrator Adare, quickly finish your business in Khoonda, and go!» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Battered Protocol Droid: «[Success] Greetings, Jedi. Welcome back to Dantooine.» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Battered Protocol Droid: «May I be of further service to you, Jedi?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Tarn: «Ah!  Leave me be, Jedi!» [K2/Khoonda_Plains] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Battered Protocol Droid: «Greetings, Jedi. May I be of any further service to you?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]
- Battered Protocol Droid: «My apologies, Jedi. May I be of any further service to you?» [K2/Khoonda_Plains]

**[K2/Kreia]**
- Bao-Dur: «Your command echoes still, General. And I obey, as I did at Malachor.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «You have taken the first steps on a much longer road, exile.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «Or perhaps you will simply cast me out, exile me - it matters not.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «It is not something he can direct or focus, much like hunger itself. He is more of a hole in the Force than a living thing. Force Sensitives and worlds rich in the Force draw him. The Miraluka world was one such place. That is why where the Jedi gather, Jedi will die. He will feel it, unless they mask their presence - but Katarr called out as a beacon to him, and he could not resist it. And he cares nothing for the Sith or its teachings... or the Jedi. And when the Jedi are dead, he will feed on the galaxy, the Republic, and eventually, consume the Sith as well.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «It is good that you have never wondered what lay beneath her robes, if her alabaster skin was as white and unblemished as her face. Or if perhaps she bore the scars of slavery... and if that would stir you more. If perhaps her deferent tone would change once you held her by the throat, and showed her how far a Jedi can fall. Few are the thoughts that can hide in the shadows of your mind, exile... and such passions are not strength, but erosion.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «We shall see. I trust your exile has taught you restraint and discipline in the ways of the flesh.» [K2/Kreia]
- Kreia: «Ah, without a home. Another exile.» [K2/Kreia]

**[K2/Landing_Rights]**
- Twin Suns: «We only wish the exile - submit, or else we shall kill you and find other bait.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Atton: «Look, we need to move. They're coming after us, not the exile.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Visas: «These hunters will be tracking the exile as well. We must warn her.» [K2/Landing_Rights] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Bao-Dur: «I'm not leaving the General behind. We either leave here together, or not at all.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Azanti Zhug: «Ah... look. Refugees. Here. On the landing pad. Are you lost? Or perhaps you have lost your criminal Jedi exile leader perhaps that is more likely, yes, very. I am Azanti Zhug, leader of Zhugs, very powerful, very skilled hunters. It would be very smart of you to tell me where the criminal Jedi has gone. And do speak very quick, my patience is very low.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Bao-Dur: «I think he wanted us to give up the General to his poorly-trained collection of bounty hunters.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Mira: «Anybody without olfactory blockers like I have is going to start feeling dizzy... and eventually fall unconscious. Good night, Jedi. Let me just take that environment suit, and I'll be back soon.» [K2/Landing_Rights]
- Visquis: «So... it seems the exile has chosen to come after all. Seal the entrance to the Jekk'Jekk Tarr. Unlock the sequencer to the private chamber, I will meet the human there.» [K2/Landing_Rights]

**[K2/Lost_Jedi]**
- Atris: «Yet they all stem from the same source. Her teachings violated the Jedi Code and lead all who listen to the dark side, as they did the exile.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- Master Vash: «You are wrong. The dark side is not what I sensed in the exile. Surely the rest of you felt it as well. That emptiness we felt... she has changed.» [K2/Lost_Jedi] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Bao-Dur: «I don't know, General. But whatever the reason, you should put it behind you.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]
- Bao-Dur: «The lightsaber isn't always a weapon of the Jedi, General. You don't need me to tell you that.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]

**[K2/Malachor_V]**
- Kreia: «Because now I understand why the exile did what she did.» [K2/Malachor_V] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Mandalore]**
- Mandalore: «War breeds butchers on both sides of the battle lines, Jedi. You know it better than anyone. It's just that we were better at it than anyone the Jedi ever faced.» [K2/Mandalore]

**[K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]**
- Mandalore: «Not yet, Jedi. Find some more to do around here. There's a jungle full of trouble out there.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalore: «It depends on who you ask. Queen Talia rules from the Royal Palace. But her cousin, General Vaklu, has a great deal of power in Iziz, as well. They disagree about many things, just like their citizens. We fought General Vaklu during the war and he is a worthy adversary. Queen Talia is young, and hardly a match for him.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Davrel: «I see now how we lost the Mandalorian Wars. You have a formidable spirit. I have no illusions that I would've been able to defeat the zakkeg without you. I doubt you even needed me. But you have helped me secure my place here. I wish you well, Jedi.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]
- Mandalore: «The gate guard said you managed to kill a zakkeg. Those are tough beasts, Jedi. You've earned some respect around here.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]

**[K2/Merchant_Quarter]**
- Captain Gelesi: «Because of the Republic attacks, General Vaklu just announced that the Office of Emigration has been shut down. There have been a lot of edicts since the Republic launched an unprovoked attack on our space forces. No one can get starport visas any more.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Gegorran: «A large variety of weapons, my stock is very good. You might want to buy some before the General decides he needs more blasters. Then weapon merchants, like myself, have the honor of providing them to him at a substantial discount. Even below what it costs us... We do it happily or else...» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Anda: «So all three Captains have been removed from their posts. You are quite impressive. Here is the reward I promised. I will let my friends know of your able assistance. I am certain we can return the favor at some point in the future. You've made a powerful ally, Jedi.» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]
- Captain Riiken: «Strength? It's not about strength. The military is being corrupted by the General. If those faithful to the Queen don't steer clear of Vaklu's kath hou-» [K2/Merchant_Quarter]

**[K2/Mira]**
- Mira: «Are you sure you're a Jedi? That exile of yours must have gone on longer than I thought. Look, you're way too old for me. Even if I were interested, you couldn't handle me anyway. I mean, you're really good in a fight. And you've got those intense eyes, and it's obvious you take care of your body, but I really don't have the luxury of getting attached to you.» [K2/Mira]

**[K2/Polar_Plateau]**
- HK-50: «Irritated Declaration: There you are. It has been extremely difficult to track you down, Jedi.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- HK-50: «Chiding Statement: Oh, Jedi, there are as many of us as are needed to capture or kill our targets.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «I did not expect to see you again after the day of your sentencing. I thought you had taken the exile's path, wandering the galaxy. Yet you have returned - why?» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «You presume much. The Force does not guide your movements, not any more. Its connection was lost when you fell. Perhaps you feel that the Force also led you to the Mandalorian Wars, as well. But in truth it only led to your exile.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «It was only a battle, exile, nothing more. You had lost the true war long ago.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «I am the last Jedi, not you. You betrayed our teachings, our beliefs... the very core of the Jedi Order.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «I said I was the last of the Jedi, exile, and I did not speak falsely. There are others who were once Jedi, but no longer. They will not take action against this threat.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Atris: «The exile reminded me of something... I had forgotten.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]
- Handmaiden: «Forgive me, mistress... but I must ask. The exile... I have never seen another effect you so strongly.» [K2/Polar_Plateau]

**[K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]**
- Master Vrook: «I see you have failed to grasp the nature of the enemy we face. Where Jedi gather, Jedi die.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Zez-Kai Ell: «The screams of countless thousands, Jedi and Mandalorians, crushed by the planet's gravity, annihilated.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «Our judgment before remains, exile. You must leave... and you must leave without your tie to the Force. It is a punishment reserved for only a few - and only when necessary, but we have the power to cut you off from the Force, and it must be done.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Master Vrook: «Is this your new Master, exile? If so, then you follow Revan's path. Her teachings will cause you to fall as surely as she did.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Kreia: «Do you wish to feel the teachings born of the Mandalorian Wars? Of all wars, of all tragedies that scream across the galaxy? Let me show you - you, who have forever seen the galaxy through the Force. See it through the eyes of the exile.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «I had thought you were the last of the Jedi, as did the Sith. But there are more of them than you know.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Kreia: «:: It is your gift to the galaxy, exile. ::» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Disciple: «I will understand if you feel you must go alone, but I ask that you do not. Instead, take strength from your connections to others. Do not forsake them, as you did in exile.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Visas: «I will understand if you feel you must go on alone, but I ask that you do not. Instead, take strength from your connection to others. Do not forsake them, as you did in exile.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]

**[K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]**
- Refugee: «Of course, of course. Tell me. What else did you want to know, Jedi?» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Refugee: «Look... Jedi, before you go. I know you probably didn't come here to save us, but... but I knew Jedi during the war, and I know that they always helped when they could. If I can help you, even just by keeping an ear out, I can let you know if I hear anything.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Twin Suns: «It is hard to wait... the beautiful exile is so close, so within reach.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]
- Twin Suns: «It is hard to wait... the handsome exile is so close, so within reach.» [K2/Refugee_Landing_Pad]

**[K2/Rescue_T3-M4]**
- HK Protocol Droid: «Explanation: Despite my market value, master, the miners were far more interested in you. It did not take long for me to ascertain the reason for this. While an HK protocol droid is a valuable piece of property, Jedi are worth much more in certain... exclusive markets across the galaxy.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]
- Atton: «Look - I could have let you rot in those mining tunnels, and all I get from you is backtalk. You know what? I don't trust you. For all I know, you could have caused all this trouble - in fact, you did, just by being a Jedi! So before you start accusing me of anything, Jedi, maybe you better take a look at what you've done.» [K2/Rescue_T3-M4]

**[K2/Residential_082_East]**
- Atton: «If she served in the war... well, Jedi are supposed to be tough. Capable.» [K2/Residential_082_East] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K2/Restoration_Zone]**
- Admiral Cede: «I see. Don't concern yourself with it too much, Lieutenant. The Republic had decided to not detain the exile anyways.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Lieutenant Grenn: «The exile? Then-» [K2/Restoration_Zone] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Admiral Onasi: «I'll tell you more when I arrive, but the Republic has decided not to detain the exile, and adopt a wait-and-see approach.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «Good to have you back, General.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «Easy now. You survived one spectacular crash. Lucky I was here to pull you and your friends out of that shuttle or you'd be more than a little crispy. But it's only fair, I owe you more than one, General.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «I'll humor you, General. I was one of the Iridonian mechanic corps that was at Malachor. Bao-Dur? I can see how you'd forget me, being that I was the only one.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «They'll be fine. The pilot's more or less unharmed and the old lady, well, she's tougher than she looks. You know, I never thought I'd see you again, General. Galaxy's a big place, and this is the last place I thought I'd bump into you. So I have to ask, just what are you doing here?» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «Got some news for you, General. That shuttle of yours is done for – scrap.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «That's cruel, General. And anyways, you'll need my help.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]
- Bao-Dur: «I don't think so. You'll have to come through me if you want to take the General.» [K2/Restoration_Zone]

**[K2/Royal_Palace]**
- Computer Terminal: «MESSAGE FROM USER 0:<br>Here's a present for you, Jedi» [K2/Royal_Palace] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Computer Terminal: «MESSAGE FROM USER 0:<br>Just try that next time, Jedi» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Computer Terminal: «MESSAGE FROM USER 0:<br>Nice try, Jedi<br><br>EXITING SYSTEM...» [K2/Royal_Palace] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Royalist Corporal: «As you wish, Jedi.» [K2/Royal_Palace] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- General Vaklu: «Damn you, Jedi. You have stopped a chance for a new age for this planet. In time Onderonians will curse your name!» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Queen Talia: «I... I just hope you're right, Jedi. Your kind are so infuriatingly wise.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «When you stood before us in the Council chamber on Coruscant, we felt something from you, we'd never felt before - it was as if the Force had died within you, leaving you hollow. We had suspicions as to why this was, but nothing definite. But rather than try to understand, we sent you away. I think because at some level, there was fear. We live our whole lives in touch with the Force, in touch with life all around us, and you had a gift in that regard. You formed bonds so easily, and they flowed deep between you and others. To see such emptiness in the Force standing before you... it is not an easy thing to face. Whatever is attacking us, it is leaving something in its wake, something we haven't felt since you stood before us in judgment. The deaths of the Jedi, the destruction of Katarr, all of these things are leaving behind echoes - like the one we felt from you in the council chamber. It was clear to us - to me, that we had to find you. But we couldn't call you back from exile, because we didn't know where you'd gone. Plus, there was a chance we might put you in danger, and that we couldn't allow. If you couldn't feel the Force, then it would just make you a target. I don't know how you got back, but I'm glad you're here.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «There was nothing else we could do. You defied the Council. You followed Revan to war. I know why you did it, but in so doing, much more harm was done. But you must understand - the exile was never the punishment you thought it to be. We could not have made you do such a thing, in any event. I think you knew, inside, what you needed to do in order to heal.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Vaklu Officer: «General Vaklu is fighting the Sith. Men, attack the Sith! Protect the General.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Master Kavar: «When you stood before us in the Council chamber on Coruscant, we felt something from you, we'd never felt before - it was as if the Force had died within you, leaving you hollow. We live our whole lives in touch with the Force, in touch with life all around us, and you had a gift in that regard. You formed bonds so easily, and they flowed deep between you and others. To see such emptiness in the Force standing before you... it's not an easy thing to face. Whatever is attacking us, it's leaving something in its wake, something we haven't felt since you stood before us in judgment. The deaths of these Jedi, the destruction of Katarr, all of these things are leaving behind echoes, like the one we felt from you in the council chamber. It was clear to us - to me, that we had to find you. But we couldn't call you back from exile, because we didn't know where you'd gone.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- General Vaklu: «Her mastery of the blade has improved. But there is more to leadership than skill with a blade. Jedi, do you want to take care of her?» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- General Vaklu: «I know who you are... You are the last of the Jedi, hunted by the Exchange, the Sith, and the Hutt-spawn knows who else. They've thrown everything they have to capture and kill you, but you cannot be stopped. If anyone could save us from our fate... it was you. After we've secured the city, and that will take time, you will always be welcome here. If the Republic hunts you down as a price of your ambition, this place will be a haven to you. I will never forget what you have done this day.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- General Vaklu: «As do I, Jedi. As do I.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Onderon Diplmat: «Hello there. I'm sorry but I must be brief, General Vaklu has ordered the immediate return of all members of the diplomatic corps.» [K2/Royal_Palace]

**[K2/Secret_Academy]**
- Handmaiden Sister: «They were caged for their safety until we could determine your intent, exile. Atris cautioned us against your tactics, fearing that your allies would create a distraction. Your companions gave us little trouble, however. The male could have presented some challenge if he had resisted, but he chose not to.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Atris has said that if Revan and Malak had been properly instructed in the ways of the Jedi, they never would have fallen... and nor would you, exile.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «No, perhaps not... if you are truly a warrior. And not some murderer without discipline. But I do not believe you are weak or a murderer. You show reflection on your actions. Perhaps I misjudged you - perhaps you have changed from the exile Atris described to me. I hope you may show her the same side you have shown me.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «If you seek to raise anger in me, you will fail, exile. I tolerate your presence only upon orders of the mistress - she did not order me to endure your questions.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «You are the exile. The one Atris warned us about.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «There is much knowledge here, and only one of the Jedi remain. There is so much about their ways of battle, their forms, their stances, that may be lost forever if the last of the Jedi is taken from the galaxy.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «[Success] That is something I had not considered. You have given me something to reflect on, exile - I thank you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «I will, exile. In truth, I do wish to challenge you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden: «Before you go, exile... question for you, if I may ask it. You have touched the Force... what does it feel like?» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Our next battle shall be free of restriction on weapons and items, and you may use whatever technique, Jedi or otherwise, you wish to attempt to defeat me. In turn, I will spare none of our forms against you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Bao-Dur: «I... am sorry, General. I must have lost consciousness in the crash.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Bao-Dur: «I am fine, General. Even power has been restored to my arm.» [K2/Secret_Academy] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Bao-Dur: «I will, General. The attack was unexpected, but I will not let down my guard again.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Bao-Dur: «I will not, General. The attack was unexpected, but I will not let down my guard again.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Bao-Dur: «Of course, General. If you wish, I may travel with you or join you at the ship.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Bao-Dur: «I am, General. If you wish I may travel with you, or join you at the ship.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Bao-Dur: «Very well, General. I will await your arrival.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Bao-Dur: «Very well, General.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atris: «She has left with the exile.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Atris: «Her reasons are unknown to me. But I fear she may no longer be trusted. We will save her if we can, but we must let her discover the exile's nature for herself. Some evils must be confronted, and isolation from it would have been no defense.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Yes, mistress: the Iridonian, the Echani-trained pilot, the exile, and the old woman.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Mistress, she was secured for much of the time the exile was here. At other times you were meditating, and we did not wish to disturb you.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Perhaps it is better that our sister chose exile rather than having it forced upon her.» [K2/Secret_Academy]
- Handmaiden Sister: «Perhaps that is why Atris sent her. Perhaps it is an exile of her own.» [K2/Secret_Academy]

**[K2/Secret_Tomb]**
- Malak: «You would... fight your fate, Jedi? Violence before reason, excellent. You make a worthy addition to our ranks.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Malak: «This is pointless then. You stand alone in this, Jedi. Soon you will see things my way.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Republic Captain: «Comm says we've lost another heavy droid transport. How can we break through the Mandalorian lines without support? The path is mined and the place is crawling with enemies. I know we've got our orders to press forward, but we're at quarter strength. We can't, General. It's impossible. We need to retreat.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Republic Captain: «We know, General. But we just don't have enough men to accomplish our objective, no matter how important.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Republic Captain: «We... we will press forward if you ask it. The path is mined. If you ask us to charge, there will be losses, General.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]
- Republic Captain: «I'll tell the men, General. Everyone, you heard the General. Charge, charge!» [K2/Secret_Tomb] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Republic Captain: «Thank you, General. Thank you.» [K2/Secret_Tomb]

**[K2/Sith_Academy]**
